# Khmer review: every UI string in English and Khmer

Generated by `npm run i18n:review` on 2026-11-11 from `src/messages/en` and `src/messages/km`. Don't edit this file by hand: run the script again after the messages change.

PhumiReader (ភូមិReader) has **2,644 UI strings** (about 11,887 English words) in 39 groups. The Khmer is a draft built from `design/i18n/km-dictionary.json`; a native Khmer speaker has to review all of it before launch (task T44, launch blocker A12). Series titles, synopses, creator names and comments are content, not UI, and are not part of this review.

## How to review

1. Read the strings page by page below, ideally with the website open in Khmer (`/km/...`) next to it.
2. Write corrections in **`docs/khmer-review.csv`** (open it in Excel or Google Sheets): put the new Khmer in the `khmer_corrected` column and anything to discuss in `comment`. Leave both empty when the draft is fine.
3. Save it as **CSV UTF-8** (Excel: *File › Save As › CSV UTF-8*; Google Sheets: *File › Download › CSV*). Other CSV types lose the Khmer letters.
4. A developer applies it with `npm run i18n:review -- --apply docs/khmer-review.csv`. The script refuses a correction whose `{placeholders}` don't match the English, and lists it.

## Rules for the Khmer text

- **Placeholders stay exactly as they are.** `{title}`, `{count}`, `{date}` are filled in by the app. Move them anywhere in the sentence, but don't translate or remove them.
- **Plural and select messages:** in `{count, plural, one {# coin} other {# coins}}` translate only the text inside the inner braces. Khmer has no plural forms, so `other {...}` alone is enough; keep `=0 {...}` or `=1 {...}` when the English has them. In `{unit, select, novel {chapter} other {episode}}` translate each branch. `#` is the number.
- **Tags** such as `<b>…</b>` or `<link>…</link>` stay; translate the words between them.
- **Numbers** (decided 2026-11-11): Khmer digits (០–៩) for counts, episode and chapter numbers, dates and times; Latin digits (0–9) for money: US dollar and riel prices such as `$4.99` or `20,000៛`. Coins count as a number, not money (`៥ កាក់`). Numbers that the app fills into a placeholder are converted automatically; numbers written into a string must follow the same rule. Strings marked “Latin digits” below contain Latin digits outside placeholders: check whether they are money.
- **Names stay as written:** PhumiReader / ភូមិReader, Fast Pass, KHQR, ABA Pay, Visa, Mastercard, Google, Facebook, and every series title and creator name.
- **Tone:** friendly and short. Buttons are actions (ចូលគណនី, ទិញកាក់), not descriptions.

## Strings that need a look first

81 strings are flagged in the **Notes** column: “same as English” (not translated yet, or a name that stays in Latin letters), “Latin digits” (see the number rule) and “placeholders differ”.

- [Navigation](#navigation--nav) (24): Header, menus and the phone tab bar

| `faq.2.a` | KHQR works with any Bakong-member bank app. You can also pay with ABA Pay or a credit or debit card. | KHQR ប្រើបានជាមួយកម្មវិធីធនាគារណាមួយដែលភ្ជាប់ Bakong។ អ្នកក៏អាចបង់ជាមួយ ABA Pay ឬកាតឥណទាន/ឥណពន្ធផងដែរ។ |  |

| `faq.3.a` | Most payments confirm in seconds. If it takes longer than 10 minutes, contact Help with your order number. | ការបង់ប្រាក់ភាគច្រើនត្រូវបានបញ្ជាក់ក្នុងរយៈពេលប៉ុន្មានវិនាទី។ ប្រសិនបើយូរជាង ១០ នាទី សូមទាក់ទងជំនួយ ជាមួយលេខការបញ្ជាទិញរបស់អ្នក។ |  |
